VIE_Change_Main_Favoritism = {
	add_to_variable = {
		var = Main_Favoritism
		value = favoritism_change
		tooltip = VIE_Favoritism_Adj_tt
	}
	refresh_favoritism_minigame = yes
}

VIE_Change_Alt_Favoritism = {
	add_to_variable = {
		var = Alt_Favoritism
		value = favoritism_change
		tooltip = VIE_Alt_Favoritism_Adj_tt
	}
	refresh_favoritism_minigame = yes
}

VIE_Change_Domestic_Influence = {
	add_to_variable = {
		var = VIE_Influence
		value = influence_change
	}
	subtract_from_variable = {
		var = FRA_Influence
		value = influence_change
	}
	custom_effect_tooltip = VIE_Influence_Adj_tt
	refresh_favoritism_minigame = yes
}

VIE_ai_french_coup_gamerule = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			is_ai = yes
			has_game_rule = { rule = VIE_ai_french_coup option = VIE_FRENCH_COUP_AI_PROTECTED }
			check_variable = { FRA_Influence < 20 }
		}
		# influence_change = FRA_Influence - 25 (negative), so FRA_Influence -> 25.
		set_temp_variable = { influence_change = FRA_Influence }
		subtract_from_temp_variable = { var = influence_change value = 25 }
		VIE_Change_Domestic_Influence = yes
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			is_ai = yes
			has_game_rule = { rule = VIE_ai_french_coup option = VIE_FRENCH_COUP_AI_GUARANTEED }
			NOT = { has_country_flag = VIE_FRA_Coup }
			check_variable = { FRA_Influence > 5 }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { influence_change = 3 }
		VIE_Change_Domestic_Influence = yes
	}
}

vietnam_cochinchina_unification_war = {
	CCC = {
		var:Sphere_Leader = {
			remove_from_array = {
				sphere_tag_list = PREV.id
			}
		}
		clr_country_flag = Is_In_Sphere
		set_variable = {
			Sphere_Leader = 0
		}
		leave_faction = yes
		drop_cosmetic_tag = yes
		VIE = {
			set_autonomy = {
				target = CCC
				autonomy_state = autonomy_free
				end_wars = no
				end_civil_wars = no
			}
		}
		TAI = {
			diplomatic_relation	= { country = CCC relation = military_access active = no }
		}
		NUN = {
			diplomatic_relation	= { country = CCC relation = military_access active = no }
		}
		every_country = {
			limit = {
				is_ally_with = FRA
			}
			add_ai_strategy = {
				type = antagonize
				id = PREV
				value = 100
			}
			CCC = {
				diplomatic_relation	= { country = PREV relation = military_access active = no }
			}
		}
	}
	VIE = {
		declare_war_on = {
			target = CCC
			type = annex_everything
		}
		every_country = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					original_tag = FUL
					original_tag = FRA
				}
			}
			add_to_war = {
				targeted_alliance = PREV.PREV
				enemy = CCC
				hostility_reason = asked_to_join
			}
		}
	}
}

VIE_diem_autocomplete_ballot_refusal = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			country_exists = VIE
			check_variable = { global.geneva_elections_term = 3 }	# immediate ballot only
			VIE = {
				has_country_flag = VIE_PM_Ngo_Dinh_Diem
				has_focus_tree = VIE_Bao_Dai_50
				NOT = { has_completed_focus = VIE_Diem_Reject_the_Geneva_Elections }
			}
		}
		set_global_flag = geneva_vie_refusal_autogranted
		VIE = { complete_national_focus = VIE_Diem_Reject_the_Geneva_Elections }
	}
}

VIE_close_favoritism_minigame = {
	VIE = {
		clr_country_flag = CWIC_HOG_UI_ENABLED
		clr_country_flag = VIE_Main_Bao_Dai
		clr_country_flag = VIE_Dealing_with_CuongDe
		reset_favoritism_minigame = yes
		set_variable = { Main_Favoritism = 0 }
	}
}

reset_favoritism_minigame = {
	VIE = {
		set_variable = { Alt_Favoritism = 5 }
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = VIE_Opposition_Cuong_De
			}
			clr_country_flag = VIE_Opposition_Cuong_De
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = VIE_Opposition_Nguyen_Van_Xuan
			}
			clr_country_flag = VIE_Opposition_Nguyen_Van_Xuan
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = VIE_Opposition_Nguyen_Phan_Long
			}
			clr_country_flag = VIE_Opposition_Nguyen_Phan_Long
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = VIE_Opposition_Tran_Van_Huu
			}
			clr_country_flag = VIE_Opposition_Tran_Van_Huu
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = VIE_Opposition_Nguyen_Van_Tam
			}
			clr_country_flag = VIE_Opposition_Nguyen_Van_Tam
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = VIE_Opposition_Nguyen_Phuc_Buu_Loc
			}
			clr_country_flag = VIE_Opposition_Nguyen_Phuc_Buu_Loc
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = VIE_Opposition_Tran_Quang_Vinh
			}
			clr_country_flag = VIE_Opposition_Tran_Quang_Vinh
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = VIE_Opposition_Nguyen_Bao_Toan
			}
			clr_country_flag = VIE_Opposition_Nguyen_Bao_Toan
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = VIE_Opposition_Dinh_Xuan_Quang
			}
			clr_country_flag = VIE_Opposition_Dinh_Xuan_Quang
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = VIE_Opposition_Diem
			}
			clr_country_flag = VIE_Opposition_Diem
		}
		refresh_favoritism_minigame = yes
	}
}

refresh_favoritism_minigame = {
	VIE = {
		add_to_variable = { VIE_Refresh_Variable = 1 }
		clamp_variable = {
			var = VIE_Influence
			min = 0
			max = 100
		}
		clamp_variable = {
			var = FRA_Influence
			min = 0
			max = 100
		}
		clamp_variable = {
			var = Main_Favoritism
			min = 0
			max = 100
		}
		clamp_variable = {
			var = Alt_Favoritism
			min = 0
			max = 100
		}
	}
	MLA = {
		add_to_variable = { MLA_Refresh_Variable = 1 }
		if = {
			limit = { has_variable = PRC_Influence }
			clamp_variable = {
				var = PRC_Influence
				min = 0
				max = 100
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_variable = CHI_Influence }
			clamp_variable = {
				var = CHI_Influence
				min = 0
				max = 100
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_variable = Main_MLA_Support }
			clamp_variable = {
				var = Main_MLA_Support
				min = 0
				max = 100
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_variable = Alt_MLA_Support }
			clamp_variable = {
				var = Alt_MLA_Support
				min = 0
				max = 100
			}
		}
	}
}

	add_to_array = {
		country_politics_list = 26	#Sectarian Menu
	}
	set_country_flag = activate_sect_menu
	country_event = VIE_Mechanics.2
}

vietnam_faction_ui_update_number = {
	set_variable = { Binh_Xuyen_Loyalty_number = 100 }
	subtract_from_variable = { Binh_Xuyen_Loyalty_number = Binh_Xuyen_Loyalty }
	set_variable = { Binh_Xuyen_Annihilation_number = 100 }
	subtract_from_variable = { Binh_Xuyen_Annihilation_number = Binh_Xuyen_Annihilation }

	set_variable = { Cao_Dai_Loyalty_number = 100 }
	subtract_from_variable = { Cao_Dai_Loyalty_number = Cao_Dai_Loyalty }
	set_variable = { Cao_Dai_Annihilation_number = 100 }
	subtract_from_variable = { Cao_Dai_Annihilation_number = Cao_Dai_Annihilation }

	set_variable = { Harmonism_Loyalty_number = 100 }
	subtract_from_variable = { Harmonism_Loyalty_number = Harmonism_Loyalty }
	set_variable = { Harmonism_Annihilation_number = 100 }
	subtract_from_variable = { Harmonism_Annihilation_number = Harmonism_Annihilation }

	set_variable = { Hoa_Hao_Loyalty_number = 100 }
	subtract_from_variable = { Hoa_Hao_Loyalty_number = Hoa_Hao_Loyalty }
	set_variable = { Hoa_Hao_Annihilation_number = 100 }
	subtract_from_variable = { Hoa_Hao_Annihilation_number = Hoa_Hao_Annihilation }

	set_variable = { UMDC_Annihilation_number = 100 }

}

VIE_annex_country = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			ROOT = {
				has_country_flag = Easy_CCC_Unification
			}
			FROM = {
				original_tag = CCC
			}
		}
		clr_country_flag = Easy_CCC_Unification
		every_owned_state = {
			limit = {
				is_core_of = FROM
			}
			set_compliance = 35
			set_resistance = 25
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		ROOT = {
			every_owned_state = {
				limit = {
					is_core_of = FROM
				}
				set_compliance = 10
				set_resistance = 50
			}
		}
	}
}

Phuong_Mutiny = {
	CCC = {
		set_country_flag = CCC_Phuong_Mutiny
		set_cosmetic_tag = CCC_Phuong
		remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = CCC_cochinchina_nam_viet_guard }
		remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = CCC_cochinchina_viet_binh_doan }
		remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = CCC_cochinchina_sect_militaries }
		transfer_state = 1285
		VIE = {
			set_province_controller = 7331
			set_province_controller = 13699
			set_province_controller = 4284
			set_province_controller = 16438
			set_province_controller = 4223
			set_province_controller = 13703
			set_province_controller = 7303
			set_province_controller = 13700
			set_province_controller = 13701
			set_province_controller = 13706
			set_province_controller = 12288
			set_province_controller = 1423
			set_province_controller = 1396
			set_province_controller = 13708
		}
		add_popularity = {
			ideology = neutrality
			popularity = 100
		}
		set_politics = {
			ruling_party = neutrality
			elections_allowed = no
			long_name = VIE_PARTY_CD_BT_QQ_long
			name = VIE_PARTY_CD_BT_QQ
		}
		CCC_Nguyen_Thanh_Phuong = {
			set_nationality = CCC
			add_field_marshal_role = {
				traits = { irregular_officer commando camouflage_expert ww2_veteran CaoDai_Member CuongDe_Comrade }
				skill = 2
				attack_skill = 1
				defense_skill = 2
				planning_skill = 1
				logistics_skill = 4
			}
			add_country_leader_role = {
				promote_leader = yes
				country_leader = {
					ideology = military_junta
				}
			}
		}
		division_template = {
			name = "Forces Armées Caodaistes"
			division_names_group = VIE_MIL_01
			template_counter = 203
			priority = 1
			is_locked = no

			regiments = {
				infantry = { x = 0 y = 0 }
				infantry = { x = 0 y = 1 }
				infantry = { x = 0 y = 2 }
				militia = { x = 1 y = 0 }
				militia = { x = 1 y = 1 }
				militia = { x = 1 y = 2 }
			}
			support = {
				engineer = { x = 0 y = 0 }
				light_artillery_support = { x = 1 y = 0 }
			}
		}
		VIE = {
			every_owned_state = {
				add_claim_by = CCC
			}
		}
		FUL = {
			every_owned_state = {
				add_claim_by = CCC
			}
		}
		declare_war_on = {
			target = VIE
			type = annex_everything
		}
		1285 = {
			create_unit = {
				division = "name = \"1st Co-Thanh Ve\" division_template = \"Forces Armées Caodaistes\" start_experience_factor = 0.2 start_equipment_factor = 0.5"
				allow_spawning_on_enemy_provs = yes
				prioritize_location = 16634
				owner = CCC
			}
			create_unit = {
				division = "name = \"2nd Co-Thanh Ve\" division_template = \"Forces Armées Caodaistes\" start_experience_factor = 0.2 start_equipment_factor = 0.5"
				allow_spawning_on_enemy_provs = yes
				prioritize_location = 16635
				owner = CCC
			}
		}
	}
}

End_Phuong_Mutiny = {
	CCC = {
		clr_country_flag = CCC_Phuong_Mutiny
		set_cosmetic_tag = CCC_FRA
		drop_cosmetic_tag = yes
		kill_country_leader = yes
		every_state = {
			limit = {
				is_claimed_by = CCC
				NOT = {
					state = 286
					state = 1285
					state = 1752
					state = 1753
					state = 1754
					state = 1755
					state = 1804
				}
			}
			remove_claim_by = CCC
		}
	}
	VIE = {
		annex_country = {
			target = CCC
			transfer_troops = no
		}
	}
}

vietnam_sect_conditions_met_check = {
	VIE = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = {
					currentMoney > 5
				}
			}
			clr_country_flag = Five_Money_Requirement_NOT_MET
			else = {
				set_country_flag = Five_Money_Requirement_NOT_MET
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = {
					currentMoney > 10
				}
			}
			clr_country_flag = Ten_Money_Requirement_NOT_MET
			else = {
				set_country_flag = Ten_Money_Requirement_NOT_MET
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = {
					currentMoney > 15
				}
			}
			clr_country_flag = Ten_Money_Requirement_NOT_MET
			else = {
				set_country_flag = Fifteen_Money_Requirement_NOT_MET
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = {
					Binh_Xuyen_Loyalty < 95
				}
			}
			clr_country_flag = Min_Binh_Xuyen_Loyalty_Requirement_NOT_MET
			else = {
				set_country_flag = Min_Binh_Xuyen_Loyalty_Requirement_NOT_MET
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = {

			clr_country_flag = Ten_Binh_Xuyen_Loyalty_Requirement_NOT_MET

VIE_dismantle_binh_xuyen = {
	set_variable = { VIE_Warlord_Status_2 = 3 }
	set_variable = { VIE_Region_16_Faction = 1 } #16 - Long An
	every_state = {
		limit = {
			has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = Binh_Xuyen_warlord_modifier }
		}
		remove_dynamic_modifier = {
			modifier = Binh_Xuyen_warlord_modifier
		}
	}
	set_country_flag = crushed_binh_xuyen
	custom_effect_tooltip = eliminate_binh_xuyen_TT
}

VIE_binh_xuyen_driven_from_saigon = {
	set_variable = { VIE_Warlord_Status_2 = 2 }	# hostile, and no longer in the capital
	set_temp_variable = { binh_xuyen_annihilation_change = -70 }	# 100 -> 30
	binh_xuyen_annihilation_change_tt = yes
	set_temp_variable = { binh_xuyen_loyalty_change = 20 }
	binh_xuyen_loyalty_change_tt = yes
	set_country_flag = VIE_Binh_Xuyen_Rung_Sac
	custom_effect_tooltip = VIE_binh_xuyen_rung_sac_tt
}

VIE_binh_xuyen_revolt_shell = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_character = VIE_Le_Van_Vien }
		VIE_Le_Van_Vien = { set_nationality = CCC }
	}
	CCC = {
		set_country_flag = CCC_Binh_Xuyen_Revolt
		set_cosmetic_tag = CCC_Binh_Xuyen
		set_politics = {
			ruling_party = neutrality
			elections_allowed = no
		}
		# The revolt is not the Cochinchina state, so strip its institutions.
		if = {
			limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = CCC_cochinchina_nam_viet_guard } }
			remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = CCC_cochinchina_nam_viet_guard }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = CCC_cochinchina_viet_binh_doan } }
			remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = CCC_cochinchina_viet_binh_doan }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = CCC_cochinchina_sect_militaries } }
			remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = CCC_cochinchina_sect_militaries }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_character = VIE_Le_Van_Vien }
			add_country_leader_role = {
				character = VIE_Le_Van_Vien
				promote_leader = yes
				country_leader = {
					ideology = stratocracy
				}
			}
		}
	}
	set_variable = { VIE_Warlord_Status_2 = 2 }	# hostile
}

VIE_create_trung_phan_commissariat = {
	custom_effect_tooltip = VIE_Trung_Phan_Commissariat_tt
	hidden_effect = {
		capital_scope = { set_state_flag = VIE_Trung_Phan_Keep_Capital }
		every_owned_state = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { has_state_flag = VIE_Trung_Phan_Keep_Capital }
				OR = {
					state = 1758
					state = 757
					state = 1759
					state = 1287
					state = 1288
					state = 1289
					state = 1756
				}
			}
			TRP = { transfer_state = PREV }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = VIE_Montagnard_Autonomy_Retained } }
			every_owned_state = {
				limit = {
					NOT = { has_state_flag = VIE_Trung_Phan_Keep_Capital }
					OR = {
						state = 1286
						state = 982
					}
				}
				TRP = { transfer_state = PREV }
			}
		}
		capital_scope = { clr_state_flag = VIE_Trung_Phan_Keep_Capital }
		if = {
			limit = { country_exists = TRP }
			set_country_flag = VIE_Trung_Phan_Commissariat
			TRP = {
				keep_current_politics = yes
				every_owned_state = { add_core_of = TRP }
				if = {
					limit = { owns_state = 1758 }
					set_capital = { state = 1758 }
				}
				else_if = {
					limit = { owns_state = 757 }
					set_capital = { state = 757 }
				}
				else = {
					random_owned_state = { save_event_target_as = trp_capital }
					set_capital = { state = event_target:trp_capital }
				}
			}
			puppet = TRP
			set_autonomy = {
				target = TRP
				autonomy_state = autonomy_indon_union
				freedom_level = 0.5
			}
			every_enemy_country = {
				TRP = {
					add_to_war = {
						targeted_alliance = VIE
						enemy = PREV
						hostility_reason = asked_to_join
					}
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { has_character = VIE_Ngo_Dinh_Can }
				VIE_Ngo_Dinh_Can = { set_nationality = TRP }
			}
			TRP = {
				set_party_name = {
					ideology = neutrality
					long_name = TRP_PARTY_NEUTRALITY_long
					name = TRP_PARTY_NEUTRALITY
				}
				set_politics = {
					ruling_party = neutrality
					elections_allowed = no
				}
				add_country_leader_role = {
					character = VIE_Ngo_Dinh_Can
					promote_leader = yes
					country_leader = {
						ideology = despotism
					}
				}
				if = {
					limit = { VIE = { has_idea = State_of_Vietnam_Faction } }
					add_ideas = State_of_Vietnam_Faction
				}
				init_influence = yes
				set_variable = { var = domestic_influence_amount value = 100 }
				add_to_array = { influence_array = VIE.id }
				add_to_array = { influence_array_val = 1800 }
				add_to_array = { influence_array = FRA.id }
				add_to_array = { influence_array_val = 400 }
				add_to_array = { influence_array = USA.id }
				add_to_array = { influence_array_val = 400 }
				set_temp_variable = { influence_total = 0 }
				set_temp_variable = { new_influence_total = 0 }
				startup_influence = yes
				if = {
					limit = { NOT = { VIE = { check_variable = { Sphere_Leader = 0 } } } }
					set_temp_variable = { New_sphere_leader = VIE.Sphere_Leader }
					add_to_sphere = yes
				}
				set_variable = { currentMoney = 0.5 }
				set_variable = { currentDebt = 0 }
				set_variable = { currentDebtPayments = 0 }
				clear_current_economic_modifiers = yes
				set_variable = { economic_x_axis = VIE.economic_x_axis }
				set_variable = { economic_y_axis = VIE.economic_y_axis }
				startup_economics = yes
				cwic_init_gdp_growth_tracking = yes
			}
		}
		# State ownership changed outside the integration GUI. Refresh its cached
		# map data immediately so the new commissariat is displayed correctly.
		VIE_state_dirty_variable = yes
	}
}

VIE_spawn_binh_xuyen_revolt = {
	VIE_binh_xuyen_revolt_shell = yes
	CCC = {
		transfer_state = 1754
		set_province_controller = 13709	# Cholon
		load_oob = CCC_Binh_Xuyen_Cholon
	}
	declare_war_on = {
		target = CCC
		type = annex_everything
	}
	custom_effect_tooltip = VIE_binh_xuyen_revolt_tt
}

VIE_spawn_binh_xuyen_revolt_full = {
	VIE_binh_xuyen_revolt_shell = yes
	CCC = {
		transfer_state = 1754
		set_province_controller = 13707	# Rung-Sac
		set_province_controller = 13709	# Cholon
		load_oob = CCC_Binh_Xuyen
	}
	declare_war_on = {
		target = CCC
		type = annex_everything
	}
	custom_effect_tooltip = VIE_binh_xuyen_revolt_tt
}

VIE_binh_xuyen_retreat_to_rung_sac = {
	VIE_binh_xuyen_driven_from_saigon = yes
	if = {
		limit = { country_exists = CCC }
		CCC = {
			set_province_controller = 13707	# Rung-Sac
			load_oob = CCC_Binh_Xuyen_RungSac
		}
	}
}

VIE_binh_xuyen_revolt_watcher = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			country_exists = CCC
			has_war_with = CCC
			CCC = {
				has_country_flag = CCC_Binh_Xuyen_Revolt
				NOT = { controls_province = 13709 }
			}
			NOT = { has_country_flag = VIE_Binh_Xuyen_Rung_Sac }
			NOT = { has_country_flag = VIE_Hoang_Dieu_Complete }
		}
		country_event = { id = Diem.42 hours = 6 }
	}
}

VIE_finish_the_binh_xuyen = {
	set_temp_variable = { binh_xuyen_annihilation_change = Binh_Xuyen_Annihilation }
	multiply_temp_variable = { binh_xuyen_annihilation_change = -1 }
	binh_xuyen_annihilation_change_tt = yes
	clr_country_flag = VIE_Binh_Xuyen_Rung_Sac
	set_country_flag = VIE_Hoang_Dieu_Complete
	VIE_dismantle_binh_xuyen = yes
	if = {
		limit = {
			country_exists = CCC
			CCC = { has_country_flag = CCC_Binh_Xuyen_Revolt }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { CCC = { has_character = VIE_Le_Van_Vien } }
			VIE_Le_Van_Vien = {
				remove_country_leader_role = { ideology = stratocracy }
				set_nationality = VIE
			}
		}
		CCC = { clr_country_flag = CCC_Binh_Xuyen_Revolt }
		annex_country = { target = CCC }
	}
}

binh_xuyen_loyalty_change_tt = {
	set_temp_variable = { inverse_number = binh_xuyen_loyalty_change }
	multiply_temp_variable = {
		inverse_number = -1
		tooltip = change_binh_xuyen_loyalty_TT
	}
	add_to_variable = { Binh_Xuyen_Loyalty = binh_xuyen_loyalty_change }
	clamp_variable = {
		var = Binh_Xuyen_Loyalty
		min = 0
		max = 100
	}
}

binh_xuyen_annihilation_change_tt = {
	set_temp_variable = { inverse_number = binh_xuyen_annihilation_change }
	multiply_temp_variable = {

		tooltip = change_binh_xuyen_annihilation_TT
	}

		max = 100
	}
}

vietnam_faction_ui_decision_click = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { vietnam_factionalism_decisions_list^i = 100 }
		}
		add_political_power = -45
		set_temp_variable = { binh_xuyen_loyalty_change = -15 }
		binh_xuyen_loyalty_change_tt = yes
		custom_effect_tooltip = VIE_Recruit_BinhXuyen_Militias_tt
		hidden_effect = {
			capital_scope = {
				create_unit = {
					division = "name = \"Cong-an Xung-phong Brigade\" division_template = \"Forces Supplétives de Binh-Xuyen\" start_experience_factor = 0.5 start_equipment_factor = 1"
					owner = VIE
				}
				create_unit = {
					division = "name = \"Cong-an Xung-phong Brigade\" division_template = \"Forces Supplétives de Binh-Xuyen\" start_experience_factor = 0.5 start_equipment_factor = 1"
					owner = VIE
				}
				create_unit = {
					division = "name = \"Cong-an Xung-phong Brigade\" division_template = \"Forces Supplétives de Binh-Xuyen\" start_experience_factor = 0.5 start_equipment_factor = 1"
					owner = VIE
				}
			}
		}
		set_country_flag = Decision_100_Completed
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { vietnam_factionalism_decisions_list^i = 101 }
		}
		set_variable = { VIE_Warlord_Status_2 = 4 }
		set_temp_variable = { binh_xuyen_loyalty_change = -8 }
		binh_xuyen_loyalty_change_tt = yes
		army_experience = 25

		limit = {
			VIE_Can_Send_Army_trigger = yes
		}
		set_variable = { VIE_Can_Send_Army = 2 }
		else = {
			set_variable = { VIE_Can_Send_Army = 1 }
		}
	}

	1752 = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				is_owned_by = VIE
				is_core_of = VIE
				is_fully_controlled_by = VIE
			}
			VIE = {
				set_variable = { VIE_State_1_Status = 2 }
				set_variable = { VIE_State_1_Owner = 1 }
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				is_owned_by = CCC
			}

				set_variable = { VIE_State_1_Owner = 2 }
			}

			limit = {
				is_owned_by = VIE
				NOT = { is_fully_controlled_by = VIE }
			}
			VIE = {
				set_variable = { VIE_State_36_Status = 7 }
				set_variable = { VIE_State_36_Owner = 1 }
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				is_owned_by = VIE
				NOT = { is_core_of = VIE }
				has_resistance = yes
				resistance > 75
				NOT = { is_controlled_by = NLF }
			}
			VIE = {
				set_variable = { VIE_State_36_Status = 6 }
				set_variable = { VIE_State_36_Owner = 1 }
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				is_owned_by = VIE
				NOT = { is_core_of = VIE }
				has_resistance = yes
				resistance < 76
				resistance > 50
				NOT = { is_controlled_by = NLF }
			}
			VIE = {
				set_variable = { VIE_State_36_Status = 5 }
				set_variable = { VIE_State_36_Owner = 1 }
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				is_owned_by = VIE
				NOT = { is_core_of = VIE }
				has_resistance = yes
				resistance < 51
				resistance > 25
				NOT = { is_controlled_by = NLF }
			}
			VIE = {
				set_variable = { VIE_State_36_Status = 4 }
				set_variable = { VIE_State_36_Owner = 1 }
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				is_owned_by = VIE
				NOT = { is_core_of = VIE }
				has_resistance = yes
				resistance < 26
				NOT = { is_controlled_by = NLF }
			}
			VIE = {
				set_variable = { VIE_State_36_Status = 3 }
				set_variable = { VIE_State_36_Owner = 1 }
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				is_owned_by = NLF
			}
			VIE = {
				set_variable = { VIE_State_36_Status = 7 }
				set_variable = { VIE_State_36_Owner = 4 }
			}
		}
	}
}

	set_variable = { VIE_M_N_FAC = -1000 }
	set_variable = { VIE_M_N_NLC = -0.10 }
	set_variable = { VIE_M_N_NIPS = -0.05 }
	set_variable = { VIE_M_N_NIC = 2 }
	set_variable = { VIE_M_N_NIP = 0.60 }
	set_variable = { VIE_M_N_AI = 0.10 }
	set_variable = { VIE_M_N_AID = 0.05 }
	set_variable = { VIE_M_N_IP = -0.20 }
	set_variable = { VIE_M_N_RS = -0.05 }
	add_dynamic_modifier = {
		modifier = VIE_etat_du_vietnam_navy
	}
}

VIE_ai_monthly_pp_relief = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			is_ai = yes
			NOT = { has_global_flag = Indochina_War_Over }
			date < 1954.1.1
			check_variable = { political_power < 50 }
		}
		add_political_power = 25
	}
}

VIE_sync_faction_name = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			tag = VIE
			is_faction_leader = yes
			NOT = { has_country_flag = VIE_generic_faction_created }
			OR = {
				NOT = { country_exists = FRE }
				FRE = { NOT = { has_country_flag = FRE_French_Indochina_Command_Established } }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_cosmetic_tag = VIE_FRA_FED }
			set_faction_name = VIE_FACTION_FED_REPUBLIC
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { has_cosmetic_tag = VIE_FRA_PROV }
			set_faction_name = VIE_FRA_PROV
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { has_cosmetic_tag = VIE_CuongDe }
			set_faction_name = VIE_FACTION_PEOPLES_STATE
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { has_cosmetic_tag = VIE_NUF }
			set_faction_name = VIE_FACTION_NUF
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					has_cosmetic_tag = VIE_ETAT
					has_cosmetic_tag = VIE_FRA_ETAT
					has_cosmetic_tag = VIE_RCB_ETAT
					has_cosmetic_tag = VIE_RCB_FRA_ETAT
					has_cosmetic_tag = VIE_CCC_ETAT
					has_cosmetic_tag = VIE_CCC_FRA_ETAT
				}
			}
			set_faction_name = VIE_FACTION_STATE_OF_VIETNAM
		}
		else = {
			set_faction_name = VIE_FACTION_REPUBLIC_OF_VIETNAM
		}
	}
}

	if = {

				recon = { x = 0 y = 1 }
				logistics_company = { x = 0 y = 2 }
				field_hospital = { x = 0 y = 3 }
				signal_company = { x = 0 y = 4 }
			}
		}
		division_template = {
			name = "Legion Etrangere Francaise Blindee"
			template_counter = 251

			is_locked = no

			regiments = {
				ffl = { x = 0 y = 0 }

# No >= in check_variable - the engine does not accept it. Compare against one less.
VIE_calculate_referendum_score = {
	set_variable = { VIE_Referendum_Score = 0 }
	add_to_variable = { VIE_Referendum_Score = Alt_Favoritism }
	subtract_from_variable = { VIE_Referendum_Score = Main_Favoritism }
	set_temp_variable = { vie_ref_tmp = VIE_Influence }
	multiply_temp_variable = { vie_ref_tmp = 0.5 }
	add_to_variable = { VIE_Referendum_Score = vie_ref_tmp }
	set_temp_variable = { vie_ref_tmp = 100 }
	subtract_from_temp_variable = { vie_ref_tmp = Cao_Dai_Loyalty }
	multiply_temp_variable = { vie_ref_tmp = 0.15 }
	add_to_variable = { VIE_Referendum_Score = vie_ref_tmp }
	set_temp_variable = { vie_ref_tmp = 100 }
	subtract_from_temp_variable = { vie_ref_tmp = Hoa_Hao_Loyalty }
	multiply_temp_variable = { vie_ref_tmp = 0.15 }
	add_to_variable = { VIE_Referendum_Score = vie_ref_tmp }
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = crushed_binh_xuyen }
		add_to_variable = { VIE_Referendum_Score = 30 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = integrated_binh_xuyen }
		add_to_variable = { VIE_Referendum_Score = 15 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = VIE_Binh_Xuyen_Rung_Sac }
		add_to_variable = { VIE_Referendum_Score = 15 }	# driven from Saigon but still alive in the swamps
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = VIE_Diem_Controls_Army }
		add_to_variable = { VIE_Referendum_Score = 25 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = VIE_Diem_Refugees_Settled }
		add_to_variable = { VIE_Referendum_Score = 15 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = VIE_Diem_NRC_Formed }
		add_to_variable = { VIE_Referendum_Score = 25 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = VIE_Can_Lao_Founded }
		add_to_variable = { VIE_Referendum_Score = 15 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = VIE_Royal_Guard_Abolished }
		add_to_variable = { VIE_Referendum_Score = 15 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = VIE_Reject_Geneva_Elections }
		add_to_variable = { VIE_Referendum_Score = 10 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = VIE_Diem_Accepted_Pham_Van_Dong }
		subtract_from_variable = { VIE_Referendum_Score = 15 }	# conceding the all-Vietnam ballot undercuts the case for a southern republic
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = VIE_Diem_Second_Cabinet_Formed }
		add_to_variable = { VIE_Referendum_Score = 10 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = VIE_Diem_Land_Redistributed }
		add_to_variable = { VIE_Referendum_Score = 10 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = VIE_Diem_Royal_Estate_Seized }
		add_to_variable = { VIE_Referendum_Score = 10 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = VIE_Diem_United_Front_Dismantled }
		add_to_variable = { VIE_Referendum_Score = 10 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = VIE_Diem_ARVN_Founded }
		add_to_variable = { VIE_Referendum_Score = 10 }
	}
	set_temp_variable = { vie_ref_tmp = VIE_DaiViet_Threat }
	multiply_temp_variable = { vie_ref_tmp = 0.25 }
	subtract_from_variable = { VIE_Referendum_Score = vie_ref_tmp }
	# Washington.
	if = {
		limit = {
			USA = { has_completed_focus = USA_50s_Permit_Diem_Regime }
		}
		add_to_variable = { VIE_Referendum_Score = 20 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_global_flag = USA_Backs_French_Coup }
		subtract_from_variable = { VIE_Referendum_Score = 30 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			is_ai = yes
			is_historical_focus_on = yes
			check_variable = { VIE_Referendum_Score < 120 }
		}
		set_variable = { VIE_Referendum_Score = 120 }
	}
	clamp_variable = {
		var = VIE_Referendum_Score
		min = 0
		max = 200
	}
}

VIE_daiviet_threat_tick = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = VIE_PM_Ngo_Dinh_Diem
			NOT = { has_global_flag = VIE_Republic_Proclaimed }
			NOT = { has_country_flag = VIE_Hinh_Coup }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { daiviet_threat_change = 3 }
		if = {
			limit = { has_global_flag = USA_Backs_French_Coup }
			add_to_temp_variable = { daiviet_threat_change = 2 }	# Washington shopping for a replacement emboldens them
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = VIE_Can_Lao_Founded }
			subtract_from_temp_variable = { daiviet_threat_change = 2 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = VIE_Ba_Long_Cleared }
			subtract_from_temp_variable = { daiviet_threat_change = 3 }
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			VIE_daiviet_threat_change = yes
		}
	}
}

VIE_diem_run_summary = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_global_flag = VIE_Republic_Proclaimed }
		custom_effect_tooltip = VIE_summary_republic_tt
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = VIE_Hinh_Coup }
		custom_effect_tooltip = VIE_summary_junta_tt
	}
	else = {
		custom_effect_tooltip = VIE_summary_throne_tt
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = VIE_Throne_Confirmed }
		custom_effect_tooltip = VIE_summary_throne_confirmed_tt
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = VIE_Hinh_Exiled }
		custom_effect_tooltip = VIE_summary_hinh_exiled_tt
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = VIE_Hinh_Bought_Off }
		custom_effect_tooltip = VIE_summary_hinh_bought_tt
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = VIE_Can_Lao_Founded }
		custom_effect_tooltip = VIE_summary_can_lao_tt
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = VIE_Hoang_Dieu_Complete }
		custom_effect_tooltip = VIE_summary_hoang_dieu_tt
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = VIE_Binh_Xuyen_Rung_Sac }
		custom_effect_tooltip = VIE_summary_rung_sac_tt
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = VIE_Ba_Long_Cleared }
		custom_effect_tooltip = VIE_summary_ba_long_tt
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = VIE_Royal_Guard_Abolished }
		custom_effect_tooltip = VIE_summary_royal_guard_tt
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = VIE_Decree_21 }
		custom_effect_tooltip = VIE_summary_decree_21_tt
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = VIE_Reject_Geneva_Elections }
		custom_effect_tooltip = VIE_summary_reject_geneva_tt
	}
	custom_effect_tooltip = VIE_50s_content_end_tt
}

VIE_cuong_de_death_check = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { has_country_flag = VIE_CuongDe_Death_Resolved }
			has_character = VIE_Cuong_De
			date > 1950.11.1
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = VIE_CuongDe_Succeeds
				date > 1952.1.1
				NOT = {
					has_country_flag = Nhiep_Successor
					has_country_flag = Diem_Successor
					has_country_flag = Hung_Successor
					has_country_flag = Vinh_Successor
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { date > 1955.1.1 }
				VIE_cuong_de_dies = yes
			}
			else = {
				random = {
					chance = 8
					VIE_cuong_de_dies = yes
				}
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					has_country_flag = VIE_CuongDe_Lost1
					AND = {
						date > 1956.1.1
						NOT = { has_country_flag = VIE_CuongDe_Succeeds }
					}
				}
			}
			VIE_cuong_de_dies = yes
		}
	}
}

VIE_cuong_de_dies = {
	set_country_flag = VIE_CuongDe_Death_Resolved
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = VIE_CuongDe_Succeeds }
		country_event = { id = CuongDe.17 hours = 6 }
	}
	else = {
		country_event = { id = BaoDai.16 hours = 6 }
	}
}

VIE_border_start_clash = {
	set_variable = { VIE_border_attacker_state = 0 }
	if = {
		limit = { event_target:VIE_border_clash_target = { is_controlled_by = ROOT } }
		set_variable = { VIE_border_attacker_state = event_target:VIE_border_clash_target }
	}
	else_if = {
		random_owned_controlled_state = {
			limit = {
				impassable = no
				has_border_war = no
				has_ROOT_at_least_1_div_in_current_state_scope = yes
				any_neighbor_state = { state = event_target:VIE_border_clash_target }
			}
			set_variable = { ROOT.VIE_border_attacker_state = THIS.id }
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { check_variable = { VIE.VIE_border_attacker_state = 0 } } }
		set_country_flag = VIE_Border_Clash_Active
		event_target:VIE_border_clash_target = { set_variable = { VIE_border_stage = 3 } }
		VIE_border_mark_aggressor = yes
		add_named_threat = {
			threat = 2
			name = VIE_border_clash_threat
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				country_exists = VIN
				event_target:VIE_border_clash_target = { owner = { is_subject_of = VIN } }
			}
			VIN = { country_event = { id = VIE_Border_Settlement.40 days = 2 } }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { VIE_border_attacker_state = event_target:VIE_border_clash_target } }
			country_event = { id = VIE_Border_Settlement.35 days = 3 }
		}
		else_if = {
			start_border_war = {
				change_state_after_war = no
				attacker = {
					state = var:ROOT.VIE_border_attacker_state
					num_provinces = 1
					on_win = VIE_Border_Settlement.30
					on_lose = VIE_Border_Settlement.31
					on_cancel = VIE_Border_Settlement.34
				}
				defender = {
					state = event_target:VIE_border_clash_target
					num_provinces = 1
					on_win = VIE_Border_Settlement.32
					on_lose = VIE_Border_Settlement.33
					on_cancel = VIE_Border_Settlement.34
				}
			}
		}
	}
	else = {
		custom_effect_tooltip = VIE_border_no_clash_tt
	}
}

VIE_border_settle_target_state = {
	var:vie_border_settle_state = {
		set_variable = { VIE_border_stage = 5 }
		set_variable = { VIE_border_pressure = 0 }
		set_variable = { VIE_border_clash_failed = 0 }
		remove_claim_by = VIE
		transfer_state_to = VIE
	}
}

VIE_border_ladder_cleanup = {	# daily failsafe - a demand whose reply never arrived would strand its state at stage 1
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = VIE_Border_Settlement_Open
			NOT = { has_country_flag = VIE_Border_Demand_Pending }
		}
		for_each_loop = {
			array = global.VIE_border_dispute_states
			value = vie_border_cleanup_state
			var:vie_border_cleanup_state = {
				if = {
					limit = { check_variable = { VIE_border_stage = 1 } }
					set_variable = { VIE_border_stage = 2 }
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

VIE_border_mark_aggressor = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_idea = VIE_Chastened_Frontier }
		swap_ideas = {
			remove_idea = VIE_Chastened_Frontier
			add_idea = VIE_Regional_Aggressor
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { has_idea = VIE_Regional_Aggressor }
			NOT = { has_idea = VIE_Regional_Hegemon }
		}
		add_ideas = VIE_Regional_Aggressor
	}
}

VIE_border_mark_hegemon = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_idea = VIE_Regional_Aggressor }
		swap_ideas = {
			remove_idea = VIE_Regional_Aggressor
			add_idea = VIE_Regional_Hegemon
		}
	}
}

VIE_border_mark_chastened = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_idea = VIE_Regional_Aggressor }
		swap_ideas = {
			remove_idea = VIE_Regional_Aggressor
			add_idea = VIE_Chastened_Frontier
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { has_idea = VIE_Regional_Hegemon }
			NOT = { has_idea = VIE_Chastened_Frontier }
		}
		add_ideas = VIE_Chastened_Frontier
	}
}

# ============================================================================
# Cuong De / Ho Chi Minh reconciliation (VIE_50s_CuongDe tree)
# ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Glue between the NV_CuongDe_Unification event chain and the Indochina
# Struggle / Geneva Conference systems. The chain no longer hand-rolls its
# own war ending: depending on when the accord lands it either de-escalates
# the struggle toward the existing Dan Quoc Peace ending
# (indochina_struggle_dan_quoc_peace), converts into negotiating capital at
# a sitting Geneva Conference, or becomes a post-partition reunification
# bid. The final merge always runs through the Dan Quoc machinery so the
# war can only end once.
# ============================================================================

# Called from NV_CuongDe_Unification.4 (VIE scope) when Hanoi has accepted
# the reunification proposal. Dispatches on the state of the wider conflict.
VIE_cuongde_unification_outcome = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_global_flag = geneva_conference_in_session }
		# The accord lands while the powers sit in Geneva: it becomes
		# negotiating capital rather than a separate peace.
		custom_effect_tooltip = VIE_cuongde_outcome_geneva_tt
		set_temp_variable = { geneva_lev_amount = 100 }
		geneva_add_vie_vin_rapprochement = yes
		set_temp_variable = { geneva_lev_amount = 50 }
		geneva_add_proindependence_leverage = yes
		hidden_effect = {
			if = { limit = { country_exists = VIE } VIE = { geneva_conf_refresh_prefs = yes } }
			if = { limit = { country_exists = VIN } VIN = { geneva_conf_refresh_prefs = yes } }
			country_event = { id = NV_CuongDe_Unification.8 days = 180 }
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { has_global_flag = Indochina_War_Over }
			country_exists = VIN
		}
		# The war is still live: the accord is an armistice, not yet a
		# merger. Guns fall silent, the diplomatic track takes over, and a
		# conference convened later starts already softened.
		custom_effect_tooltip = VIE_cuongde_outcome_armistice_tt
		if = {
			limit = {
				country_exists = VIN
				has_war_with = VIN
			}
			white_peace = VIN
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				country_exists = NLF
				has_war_with = NLF
			}
			white_peace = NLF
		}
		indochina_struggle_add_pro_independence_diplomatic_points = yes
		indochina_struggle_add_pro_independence_diplomatic_points = yes
		set_temp_variable = { geneva_lev_amount = 100 }
		geneva_add_vie_vin_rapprochement = yes
		hidden_effect = {
			news_event = { id = NV_CuongDe_Unification.5 days = 1 }
			country_event = { id = NV_CuongDe_Unification.8 days = 180 }
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { country_exists = VIN }
		# The struggle already closed with two Vietnams standing (Geneva
		# partition, a negotiated peace, or a frozen conflict): the accord
		# is a reunification bid between two states at peace.
		custom_effect_tooltip = VIE_cuongde_outcome_postwar_tt
		add_opinion_modifier = {
			target = VIN
			modifier = large_increase
		}
		VIN = {
			add_opinion_modifier = {
				target = VIE
				modifier = large_increase
			}
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			news_event = { id = NV_CuongDe_Unification.5 days = 1 }
			country_event = { id = NV_CuongDe_Unification.8 days = 180 }
		}
	}
	else = {
		# The North no longer exists; there is nobody left to reconcile with.
		custom_effect_tooltip = VIE_cuongde_outcome_moot_tt
	}
}

# Called from NV_CuongDe_Unification.8 (VIE scope) when the transition
# period ends. Performs the actual merger through the Dan Quoc Peace
# machinery - or aborts if the men who signed the accord are gone.
VIE_cuongde_finalize_unification = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_global_flag = geneva_conference_in_session }
		# Never conclude while the powers are still at the table; try again
		# once the conference has risen.
		custom_effect_tooltip = VIE_cuongde_finalize_deferred_tt
		hidden_effect = {
			country_event = { id = NV_CuongDe_Unification.8 days = 30 }
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { has_global_flag = diem_ho_chi_minh_reunified }
		# Some other road already led to the same reunified Vietnam.
		custom_effect_tooltip = VIE_cuongde_finalize_moot_tt
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			country_exists = VIN
			indochina_dan_quoc_peace_cond_diem = yes
			indochina_dan_quoc_peace_cond_ho_chi_minh = yes
			NOT = { has_war_with = VIN }
		}
		custom_effect_tooltip = VIE_cuongde_finalize_merge_tt
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { has_global_flag = Indochina_War_Over }
			}
			# The struggle is still formally open: let the Dan Quoc Peace
			# ending close it. With diem_ho_chi_minh_reunified set first,
			# the effect performs the merge, the white peaces, the cleanup
			# and the Indochina_Struggle_Ending.5 news itself.
			hidden_effect = {
				set_global_flag = diem_ho_chi_minh_reunified
			}
			indochina_struggle_dan_quoc_peace = yes
		}
		else = {
			# The war was already closed (Geneva partition or an earlier
			# peace); only the merger itself remains. Mirrors the merge
			# branch of indochina_struggle_dan_quoc_peace.
			VIE = {
				transfer_state = 838
				transfer_state = 786
				transfer_state = 671
				transfer_state = 881
				transfer_state = 1280
				transfer_state = 1281
				transfer_state = 1760
				transfer_state = 1761
				transfer_state = 1766
				annex_country = {
					target = VIN
					transfer_troops = yes
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						NOT = {
							has_country_leader = {
								character = VIE_Ngo_Dinh_Diem
								ruling_only = yes
							}
						}
					}
					add_country_leader_role = {
						character = VIE_Ngo_Dinh_Diem
						promote_leader = yes
						country_leader = {
							ideology = oriental_personalism
						}
					}
				}
				remove_ideas = State_of_Vietnam_Faction
			}
			set_global_flag = diem_ho_chi_minh_reunified
		}
	}
	else = {
		# Diem or Ho is gone, or the guns speak again: the moment has passed.
		hidden_effect = {
			country_event = { id = NV_CuongDe_Unification.9 hours = 6 }
		}
		custom_effect_tooltip = VIE_cuongde_finalize_failed_tt
	}
}

# Shared reward for the reconciliation press events: warmth between the two
# Vietnams plus diplomatic de-escalation of the struggle. Amount of
# rapprochement is passed in temp variable cuongde_rapprochement_gain.
VIE_cuongde_thaw_effect = {
	indochina_struggle_add_pro_independence_diplomatic_points = yes
	indochina_struggle_add_communist_diplomatic_points = yes
	set_temp_variable = { geneva_lev_amount = cuongde_rapprochement_gain }
	geneva_add_vie_vin_rapprochement = yes
	if = {
		limit = { country_exists = VIE }
		VIE = {
			add_opinion_modifier = {
				target = VIN
				modifier = small_increase
			}
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = { country_exists = VIN }
		VIN = {
			add_opinion_modifier = {
				target = VIE
				modifier = small_increase
			}
		}
	}
}

# Souring effect: the reconciliation curdles. The leverage API clamps
# non-positive amounts back to +50, so negative rapprochement is written
# directly.
VIE_cuongde_chill_effect = {
	add_to_variable = { global.geneva_rapprochement_vie_vin = cuongde_rapprochement_loss }
	custom_effect_tooltip = geneva_sub_vie_vin_rapprochement_TT
}
